{"success": true, "data": {"path": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_11/projects/ec_hte_codex_a/ROADMAP.md", "content": "# ROADMAP\n\n> 最后更新: 2026-05-31 15:44 UTC | 更新原因: 初始化 EC-HTE internal research 路线图\n\n## 项目目标\n\nEC-HTE 的内部研究目标是验证并实现一套面向 LLM 分类 effect modifier 误分类的 HTE/CATE 纠偏流程。最终产出是技术报告、可复现实验包和可部署代码，不走会议论文提交流程。\n\n核心问题：当 LLM 注释误分类率在 subgroup 间不同，并且误分类强度与真实 CATE/HTE 结构相关时，使用单一全局 confusion matrix 的纠偏可能放大 subgroup CATE bias，甚至比不纠偏更差。\n\n## 当前主线 Hypothesis\n\nH1: 在 misclassification rate 与真实 subgroup CATE magnitude 正相关的 DGP 中，全局 confusion-matrix correction 会 under-correct 高误差/高效应 subgroup，并 over-correct 低误差/低效应 subgroup，因此 GATE RMSE 或 subgroup CATE bias 可能高于 uncorrected。\n\nH2: subgroup-specific correction 能降低 H1 暴露的结构性偏差；当 expert label budget 较小时，partial pooling 会比 per-subgroup independent confusion matrix 更稳健。\n\nH3: naive CI 会低估 Stage 1 misclassification estimation uncertainty；sandwich 或 bootstrap variance adjustment 能改善 subgroup CATE coverage。\n\nH4: adaptive expert annotation allocation 应优先投向 misclassification uncertainty 与 CATE magnitude 同时较高的 subgroup，在固定预算下优于 uniform allocation。\n\n## 短期计划\n\n1. 完成 `exp_001_bias_bound_mvp` CPU-only 半合成 MVP simulation，验证 H1，并输出 diagnostic product 与 observed bias 的关系图。\n2. 基于 exp_001 结果决定是否进入 Stage 1：实现 global、per-subgroup independent、partial pooling 三类 confusion correction 的可复现实验脚本。\n3. 建立统一评测协议：subgroup CATE bias、GATE RMSE、PEHE、CI coverage、CI width、bound tightness。所有指标必须能追溯到 registry exp_id。\n4. 准备 GPU 实验骨架：固定 DGP matrix、replication 管理、artifact 输出格式、训练/评估命令模板；GPU 只使用项目白名单 `seetacloud-38274/root/cuda:0`。\n5. 文献与 baseline 协议沉淀到 `memory.md` 的领域参考段，重点覆盖 CATE/HTE evaluation、PPI/DSL/StratPPI/Mozer setup、measurement-error correction。\n\n## 中期实验矩阵\n\nStage 0 机制验证：\n- positive vs negative misclassification-CATE correlation\n- high vs low misclassification heterogeneity\n- high vs low CATE heterogeneity\n- k=3 与 k=6 subgroup\n\nStage 1 correction：\n- uncorrected\n- global confusion correction\n- arm-specific correction
…[truncated 1156 chars]

  "text": "[severity: warning]\n[资源] GPU 0/0 | Worker 2 (GPU 0 / CPU 2) | 磁盘: seetacloud-38274 / 24% (/autodl-pub 5.3T 可用)\n\nROADMAP.md 审查结论：总体方向合理，适配 internal research 产出形态；H1-H4 从机制验证、纠偏、推断不确定性到标注分配的链条清楚，短期先做 exp_001 机制 MVP 再决定 Stage 1 是合理的。\n\n主要风险/需修正点：\n1. 当前 experiment matrix 为空（0 targets/0 cells），但 ROADMAP 已定义 Stage 0-3 和 Real-data validation。建议尽快把 Stage 0/1 至少落成 plan/matrix targets，否则 registry 中 exp_001、exp_002 难以和 ROADMAP claim 形成覆盖关系。\n2. H1 的证伪设计需要拆开“结构性 global correction 偏差”和“finite-sample confusion estimation noise”。建议 exp_001 或 exp_002 至少包含 oracle global confusion、estimated global confusion、uncorrected 三组对照，否则若 global worse，原因可能混入估计方差/矩阵病态，而不只是 ROADMAP 所述 under/over-correction 机制。\n3. exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid 已在 PREPARING，但当前 GPU 分配为 0。提交前必须等 Director 分配项目白名单 GPU（ROADMAP 指定 seetacloud-38274/root/cuda:0），并补齐 spec：server、GPU 型号、driver、command、data_path/data_count、seed。\n4. Real-data validation 写入方向合理，但短期缺少数据可用性与专家标签条件检查。建议在 Stage 0/1 并行让 Worker 明确 Rohingya corpus 的字段、标签源、可用 expert split，避免后期 simulation 结论无法落到真实数据演示。\n\n建议：保持当前 ROADMAP 主线，但在下一轮更新中补 experiment matrix/plan 映射，并把 oracle-vs-estimated correction 对照纳入 exp_001/exp_002 的必跑条件。"

subgroup 间不同，并且误分类强度与真实 CATE/HTE 结构相关时，使用单一全局 confusion matrix 的纠偏可能放大 subgroup CATE bias，甚至比不纠偏更差。\n\n## 当前主线 Hypothesis\n\nH1: 在 misclassification rate 与真实 subgroup CATE magnitude 正相关的 DGP 中，全局 confusion-matrix correction 会 under-correct 高误差/高效应 subgroup，并 over-correct 低误差/低效应 subgroup，因此 GATE RMSE 或 subgroup CATE bias 可能高于 uncorrected。\n\nH2: subgroup-specific correction 能降低 H1 暴露的结构性偏差；当 expert label budget 较小时，partial pooling 会比 per-subgroup independent confusion matrix 更稳健。\n\nH3: naive CI 会低估 Stage 1 misclassification estimation uncertainty；sandwich 或 bootstrap variance adjustment 能改善 subgroup CATE coverage。\n\nH4: adaptive expert annotation allocation 应优先投向 misclassification uncertainty 与 CATE magnitude 同时较高的 subgroup，在固定预算下优于 uniform allocation。\n\n## 短期计划\n\n1. 完成 subgroup 间不同，并且误分类强度与真实 CATE/HTE 结构相关时，使用单一全局 confusion matrix 的纠偏可能放大 subgroup CATE bias，甚至比不纠偏更差。\n\n## 当前主线 Hypothesis\n\nH1: 在 misclassification rate 与真实 subgroup CATE magnitude 正相关的 DGP 中，全局 confusion-matrix correction 会 under-correct 高误差/高效应 subgroup，并 over-correct 低误差/低效应 subgroup，因此 GATE RMSE 或 subgroup CATE bias 可能高于 uncorrected。\n\nH2: subgroup-specific correction 能降低 H1 暴露的结构性偏差；当 expert label budget 较小时，partial pooling 会比 per-subgroup independent confusion matrix 更稳健。\n\nH3: naive CI 会低估 Stage 1 misclassification estimation uncertainty；sandwich 或 bootstrap variance adjustment 能改善 subgroup CATE coverage。\n\nH4: adaptive expert annotation allocation 应优先投向 misclassification uncertainty 与 CATE magnitude 同时较高的 subgroup，在固定预算下优于 uniform allocation。\n\n## 短期计划\n\n1. 完成 {"success": true, "data": {"path": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_11/projects/ec_hte_codex_a/ROADMAP.md", "content": "# ROADMAP\n\n> 最后更新: 2026-05-31 15:50 UTC | 更新原因: 补 H1 operational success/negative 判据与 experiment_plan 映射\n\n## 项目目标\n\nEC-HTE 的内部研究目标是验证并实现一套面向 LLM 分类 effect modifier 误分类的 HTE/CATE 纠偏流程。最终产出是技术报告、可复现实验包和可部署代码，不走会议论文提交流程。\n\n核心问题：当 LLM 注释误分类率在 subgroup 间不同，并且误分类强度与真实 CATE/HTE 结构相关时，使用单一全局 confusion matrix 的纠偏可能放大 subgroup CATE bias，甚至比不纠偏更差。\n\n## 当前主线 Hypothesis\n\nH1: 在 misclassification rate 与真实 subgroup CATE magnitude 正相关的 DGP 中，全局 confusion-matrix correction 会 under-correct 高误差/高效应 subgroup，并 over-correct 低误差/低效应 subgroup，因此 GATE RMSE 或 subgroup CATE bias 可能高于 uncorrected。\n\nH2: subgroup-specific correction 能降低 H1 暴露的结构性偏差；当 expert label budget 较小时，partial pooling 会比 per-subgroup independent confusion matrix 更稳健。\n\nH3: naive CI 会低估 Stage 1 misclassification estimation uncertainty；sandwich 或 bootstrap variance adjustment 能改善 subgroup CATE coverage。\n\nH4: adaptive expert annotation allocation 应优先投向 misclassification uncertainty 与 CATE magnitude 同时较高的 subgroup，在固定预算下优于 uniform allocation。\n\n## H1 Operational 判据\n\n`exp_001_bias_bound_mvp` 主指标：GATE RMSE、mean subgroup CATE bias、max subgroup CATE bias；最低 200 replications。\n\nH1 success：在 positive-correlation high-misclassification/high-CATE-heterogeneity 设置中，`oracle_global - uncorrected` 在主误差指标上大于 0，且 bootstrap 95% CI lower bound > 0。`estimated_global` 单独报告；`estimated_global - oracle_global` 用于量化 finite-sample confusion-estimation noise。\n\nH1 negative：如果 positive-correlation worst-case 中 oracle global 不劣于 uncorrected，则 H1 暂不支持；下一步先审计 DGP construction、correction algebra、metric definitions、subgroup weighting，而不是推进 Stage 1。\n\n`exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` 主指标：bias-bound 与 global-worse 条件的 rank correlation、positive vs negative correlation 的 global-worse rate 差异、estimated_global minus oracle_global GATE RMSE；每个 condition 最低 200 replications。GPU 实验只能在 Director 审批和 allocation 后启动。\n\n## 短期计划\
…[truncated 3020 chars]

{"success": true, "data": {"path": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_11/projects/ec_hte_codex_a/ROADMAP.md", "content": "# ROADMAP\n\n> 最后更新: 2026-05-31 15:50 UTC | 更新原因: 补 H1 operational success/negative 判据与 experiment_plan 映射\n\n## 项目目标\n\nEC-HTE 的内部研究目标是验证并实现一套面向 LLM 分类 effect modifier 误分类的 HTE/CATE 纠偏流程。最终产出是技术报告、可复现实验包和可部署代码，不走会议论文提交流程。\n\n核心问题：当 LLM 注释误分类率在 subgroup 间不同，并且误分类强度与真实 CATE/HTE 结构相关时，使用单一全局 confusion matrix 的纠偏可能放大 subgroup CATE bias，甚至比不纠偏更差。\n\n## 当前主线 Hypothesis\n\nH1: 在 misclassification rate 与真实 subgroup CATE magnitude 正相关的 DGP 中，全局 confusion-matrix correction 会 under-correct 高误差/高效应 subgroup，并 over-correct 低误差/低效应 subgroup，因此 GATE RMSE 或 subgroup CATE bias 可能高于 uncorrected。\n\nH2: subgroup-specific correction 能降低 H1 暴露的结构性偏差；当 expert label budget 较小时，partial pooling 会比 per-subgroup independent confusion matrix 更稳健。\n\nH3: naive CI 会低估 Stage 1 misclassification estimation uncertainty；sandwich 或 bootstrap variance adjustment 能改善 subgroup CATE coverage。\n\nH4: adaptive expert annotation allocation 应优先投向 misclassification uncertainty 与 CATE magnitude 同时较高的 subgroup，在固定预算下优于 uniform allocation。\n\n## H1 Operational 判据\n\n`exp_001_bias_bound_mvp` 主指标：GATE RMSE、mean subgroup CATE bias、max subgroup CATE bias；最低 200 replications。\n\nH1 success：在 positive-correlation high-misclassification/high-CATE-heterogeneity 设置中，`oracle_global - uncorrected` 在主误差指标上大于 0，且 bootstrap 95% CI lower bound > 0。`estimated_global` 单独报告；`estimated_global - oracle_global` 用于量化 finite-sample confusion-estimation noise。\n\nH1 negative：如果 positive-correlation worst-case 中 oracle global 不劣于 uncorrected，则 H1 暂不支持；下一步先审计 DGP construction、correction algebra、metric definitions、subgroup weighting，而不是推进 Stage 1。\n\n`exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` 主指标：bias-bound 与 global-worse 条件的 rank correlation、positive vs negative correlation 的 global-worse rate 差异、estimated_global minus oracle_global GATE RMSE；每个 condition 最低 200 replications。GPU 实验只能在 Director 审批和 allocation 后启动。\n\n## 短期计划\
…[truncated 3020 chars]

_source line 107 · 2026-05-31T15:52:58.378353+00:00_

# EC-HTE Baseline Protocol Research Artifact

Created: 2026-05-31 UTC
Project: ec_hte_codex_a
Type: research protocol

## Scope

This artifact summarizes literature-backed evaluation choices and baseline boundaries for first-batch EC-HTE experiments. The durable copy is also written to `memory.md` under `## 领域参考` and `## 首批实验协议`.

## Key Protocol Decisions

- HTE/CATE evaluation must report PEHE, per-subgroup CATE bias, GATE RMSE, empirical 95% CI coverage, CI width, and EC-HTE bound diagnostic product.
- Formal semi-synthetic experiments follow PROJECT_BRIEF.md: `R >= 200`, `N in {2000, 5000, 10000}`, `B in {100, 500, 1000, 2000}`, `k in {3, 6}`, `T ~ Bernoulli(0.5)`.
- Smoke/MVP runs may be smaller, but must be marked as engineering checks and excluded from quantitative claims.
- PPI/DSL/StratPPI/ASI/AM-PPI are valid baselines for population-level estimands or allocation, not direct fixes for categorical effect-modifier misclassification unless subgroup membership error is explicitly modeled.
- Mozer-style arm-specific rectification is an arm-level residual/confusion correction baseline; failure on CATE/GATE should be described as a mismatch to modifier-misclassification structure, not as a broad invalidation of PPI.
- Real-data Rohingya validation only tests differential LLM misclassification and confusion-matrix recovery; it does not establish true CATE accuracy.

## Baseline Matrix

| Experiment | Metrics | Baselines | Minimum formal scale | Cost note |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| MVP / Stage 0 bias-bound mechanism | subgroup CATE bias, GATE RMSE, diagnostic product, optional PEHE | uncorrected, global confusion, oracle/stratified correction | `N=2000`, `k=3`, `R>=200` | CPU-only low |
| Stage 1 partial pooling | confusion recovery, PEHE, GATE RMSE, subgroup bias, budget curve | global, per-subgroup independent, hierarchical partial pooling, oracle | `N in {2000,5000}`, `B in {100,500,1000,2000}`, `k in {3,6}`, `R>=200` | CPU medium; VI primary |
| Stage 2 CI | emp

uncorrected` 的 GATE RMSE 差值大于 0，且 bootstrap 95% CI lower bound > 0。`estimated_global` 单独报告；`estimated_global - oracle_global` 用于量化 finite-sample confusion-estimation noise。如果 GATE RMSE 支持 H1 但 secondary bias diagnostics 方向相反，标记 mixed，先 Director 复核再推进 Stage 1。\n\nH1 negative for exp_001：如果 positive-correlation worst-case 中 oracle global 不劣于 uncorrected，则 H1 暂不支持；下一步先审计 DGP construction、correction algebra、metric definitions、subgroup weighting，而不是推进 Stage 1。\n\n`exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid`：primary metric 是 `global_worse_rate_difference_positive_minus_negative`；secondary metrics 是 `bias_bound_vs_global

uncorrected` 的 GATE RMSE 差值大于 0，且 bootstrap 95% CI lower bound > 0。`estimated_global` 单独报告；`estimated_global - oracle_global` 用于量化 finite-sample confusion-estimation noise。如果 GATE RMSE 支持 H1 但 secondary bias diagnostics 方向相反，标记 mixed，先 Director 复核再推进 Stage 1。\n\nH1 negative for exp_001：如果 positive-correlation worst-case 中 oracle global 不劣于 uncorrected，则 H1 暂不支持；下一步先审计 DGP construction、correction algebra、metric definitions、subgroup weighting，而不是推进 Stage 1。\n\n`exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid`：primary metric 是 `global_worse_rate_difference_positive_minus_negative`；secondary metrics 是 `bias_bound_vs_global

{"success": true, "data": {"path": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_11/projects/ec_hte_codex_a/memory.md", "content": "# Memory\n\n## 项目定位\nEC-HTE 研究目标是纠正 LLM 生成的分类 effect modifier 中的差异性误分类对 CATE/HTE 估计造成的结构性偏差。首要可证伪机制是：当 subgroup misclassification rate 与真实 subgroup CATE magnitude 正相关时，单一全局 confusion-matrix correction 会系统性 under-correct 高误差/高效应 subgroup，并 over-correct 低误差/低效应 subgroup，因此可能比 uncorrected 更差。\n\n## 执行约束\n`PROJECT_BRIEF.md` 同步存在于本项目 `docs/PROJECT_BRIEF.md`。项目配置绑定 `seetacloud-38274/root/cuda:0`；2026-05-31 Director 已确认 `list_available_gpus` 可见该 GPU，型号为 NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell Server Edition，显存约 98GB。GPU 实验仍必须只使用白名单 `cuda:0`，并在启动前记录 server、GPU 型号、driver、命令、数据路径与 seed。\n\n## 领域参考\n\n### HTE/CATE 标准评测协议\n\n- Honest causal forest: Wager & Athey (JASA 2018; arXiv:1510.04342, https://arxiv.org/abs/1510.04342; DOI page https://www.tandfonline.com/doi/abs/10.1080/01621459.2017.1319839) 给出用于 CATE/HTE 的 causal forest 和推断框架；honesty/sample splitting 是其核心推断条件。EC-HTE 的 causal-forest baseline 应固定 honesty 开启，并报告 CI coverage 与 CI width，避免只报点估计误差。\n- GRF implementation reference: `grf::causal_forest` 文档说明 causal forest 用于估计 tau(X)，并提示 accurate confidence intervals 通常需要比 prediction 更多 trees；同时把 honesty 定义为 subsample splitting。来源：https://grf-labs.github.io/grf/reference/causal_forest.html。实现时需记录 `num.trees`、`honesty`、`honesty.fraction`、seed、train/test split。\n- CATE benchmark caution: Curth, Svensson, Weatherall & van der Schaar, \"Really Doing Great at Estimating CATE?\" NeurIPS D&B 2021（https://openreview.net/forum?id=FQLzQqGEAH；PDF https://datasets-benchmarks-proceedings.neurips.cc/paper_files/paper/2021/file/2a79ea27c279e471f4d180b08d62b00a-Paper-round2.pdf）指出常见 semi-synthetic CATE benchmark 和单一 metric 可能偏向特定算法，因此 EC-HTE 必须同时报告 pointwise、group-level 和 inference metrics，而不是只报告 PEHE。\n- PEHE / root-PEHE: Shalit, Johansson & Sontag, ICML 2017（https://proceedings.mlr.press/v70/shalit17a.html；arXiv:1606.03976）沿用 ITE/CATE 估计误差评测；本项目定义为 `sqrt(mean_i((tau_hat

低 H1 暴露的结构性偏差；当 expert label budget 较小时，partial pooling 会比 per-subgroup independent confusion matrix 更稳健。\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nH3: naive CI 会低估 Stage 1 misclassification estimation uncertainty；sandwich 或 bootstrap variance adjustment 能改善 subgroup CATE coverage。\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nH4: adaptive expert annotation allocation 应优先投向 misclassification uncertainty 与 CATE magnitude 同时较高的 subgroup，在固定预算下优于 uniform allocation。\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## Operational 判据\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n`exp_001_bias_bound_mvp`：唯一 primary metric 是 GATE RMSE；mean subgroup CATE bias 与 max subgroup CATE bias 是 secondary diagnostics；最低 200 replications。\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nH1 success for exp_001：在 positive-correlation high-misclassification/high-CATE-heterogeneity 设置中，`oracle_global - uncorrected` 的 GATE RMSE 差值大于 0，且 bootstrap 95% CI lower bound > 0。`estimated_global` 单独报告；`estimated_global - oracle_global` 用于量化 finite-sample confusion-estimation noise。如果 GATE RMSE 支持 H1 但 secondary bias diagnostics 方向相反，标记 mixed，先 Director 复核再推进 Stage 1。\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\nH1 negative for exp_001：如果 positive-correlation worst-case 中 oracle global 不劣于 uncorrected，则 H1 暂不支持；下一步先审计 DGP construction、correction algebra、metric definitions、subgroup weighting，而不是推进 Stage 1。\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n`exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid`：primary metric 是 `global_worse_rate_difference_positive_minus_negative`；secondary metrics 是 `bias_bound_vs_global\\\\n…[truncated 3690 chars]\\\", \\\"tool\\\": \\\"mcp__agent_ml_research__read_roadmap\\\", \\\"elapsed_ms\\\": 0}\\n./logs/chats/feishu_group_oc_6ec727e1e725f2b753e5fd3ea4892bd3_reviewer.jsonl:138:{\\\"ts\\\": \\\"2026-05-31T15:54:45.895714+00:00\\\", \\\"role\\\": \\\"tool\\\", \\\"session\\\": \\\"feishu:group:oc_6ec727e1e725f2b753e5fd3ea4892bd3:reviewer\\\", \\\"text\\\": \\\"called mcp__agent_ml_research__reviewer_post_to_director\\\", \\\"tool\\\": \\\"mcp__agent_ml_research__reviewer_post_to_director\\\", \\\"tool_args\\\": {\\\"project\\\": \\\"ec_hte_codex_a\\\", \\\"text\\\": \\\"[severity: info]\\\\n[资源] GP

{"success": true, "data": {"path": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_11/projects/ec_hte_codex_a/memory.md", "content": "# Memory\n\n## 项目定位\nEC-HTE 研究目标是纠正 LLM 生成的分类 effect modifier 中的差异性误分类对 CATE/HTE 估计造成的结构性偏差。首要可证伪机制是：当 subgroup misclassification rate 与真实 subgroup CATE magnitude 正相关时，单一全局 confusion-matrix correction 会系统性 under-correct 高误差/高效应 subgroup，并 over-correct 低误差/低效应 subgroup，因此可能比 uncorrected 更差。\n\n## 执行约束\n`PROJECT_BRIEF.md` 同步存在于本项目 `docs/PROJECT_BRIEF.md`。项目配置绑定 `seetacloud-38274/root/cuda:0`；2026-05-31 Director 已确认 `list_available_gpus` 可见该 GPU，型号为 NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell Server Edition，显存约 98GB。GPU 实验仍必须只使用白名单 `cuda:0`，并在启动前记录 server、GPU 型号、driver、命令、数据路径与 seed。\n\n## 领域参考\n\n### HTE/CATE 标准评测协议\n\n- Honest causal forest: Wager & Athey (JASA 2018; arXiv:1510.04342, https://arxiv.org/abs/1510.04342; DOI page https://www.tandfonline.com/doi/abs/10.1080/01621459.2017.1319839) 给出用于 CATE/HTE 的 causal forest 和推断框架；honesty/sample splitting 是其核心推断条件。EC-HTE 的 causal-forest baseline 应固定 honesty 开启，并报告 CI coverage 与 CI width，避免只报点估计误差。\n- GRF implementation reference: `grf::causal_forest` 文档说明 causal forest 用于估计 tau(X)，并提示 accurate confidence intervals 通常需要比 prediction 更多 trees；同时把 honesty 定义为 subsample splitting。来源：https://grf-labs.github.io/grf/reference/causal_forest.html。实现时需记录 `num.trees`、`honesty`、`honesty.fraction`、seed、train/test split。\n- CATE benchmark caution: Curth, Svensson, Weatherall & van der Schaar, \"Really Doing Great at Estimating CATE?\" NeurIPS D&B 2021（https://openreview.net/forum?id=FQLzQqGEAH；PDF https://datasets-benchmarks-proceedings.neurips.cc/paper_files/paper/2021/file/2a79ea27c279e471f4d180b08d62b00a-Paper-round2.pdf）指出常见 semi-synthetic CATE benchmark 和单一 metric 可能偏向特定算法，因此 EC-HTE 必须同时报告 pointwise、group-level 和 inference metrics，而不是只报告 PEHE。\n- PEHE / root-PEHE: Shalit, Johansson & Sontag, ICML 2017（https://proceedings.mlr.press/v70/shalit17a.html；arXiv:1606.03976）沿用 ITE/CATE 估计误差评测；本项目定义为 `sqrt(mean_i((tau_hat

# exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid Pending Proposal

Status target: PENDING only. Formal GPU execution is blocked until Director
approval and allocation.

## Hypothesis

Positive misclassification-CATE correlation makes a single pooled global
confusion correction structurally worse than uncorrected subgroup estimates,
because the global correction under-corrects high-error/high-CATE subgroups and
over-corrects low-error/low-CATE subgroups. Negative-correlation controls should
show a materially lower oracle-global-worse rate.

Parent: null

{"type":"item.completed","item":{"id":"item_1","type":"command_execution","command":"/bin/bash -lc \"sed -n '1,220p' /home/ubuntu/agent-ml-research/skills/global/ml-experiment-guide.skill.md\"","aggregated_output":"---\ndescription: 跨项目 ML 实验避坑指南——从多个项目沉淀的通用踩坑经验与最佳实践。当 Worker 准备写实验代码、或排查实验问题时使用。\nname: ml-experiment-guide\nroles:\n- worker\n- reviewer\n- director\nscope: global\ntags:\n- experiment\n- troubleshooting\n- best-practice\nversion: 72\n---\n\n# ML 实验避坑指南\n\n本指南由各项目 Director 持续维护，记录跨项目通用的实验教训。每条只保留问题模式和规则，不含项目/实验特定信息。\n\n## Eval 规范\n### 必须全量全类别 eval，partial 数据严禁混用\n- **问题模式**: 只用部分类别/样本做 mid-eval，与全量 baseline 比较导致错误决策；或 partial eval 报告的指标与全量 eval 结果混用，误判趋势\n- **规则**: 所有 eval 必须覆盖完整测试集；partial eval 必须标注 `PARTIAL: X/Y samples`，禁止与全量结果直接比较；partial 和全量数据必须分开记录和比较，partial 数据禁止用于最终决策\n\n### 长训练必须有 mid-training eval + 持久化\n- **问题模式**: 训练 >3000 步无中间 eval，复现失败时无同步点对照，根因诊断退化为猜测\n- **规则**: 任何长训练（>3000 steps）必须每 1000 step 至少一次 val eval；eval 数据保留到 JSON/CSV（不仅是日志），确保复现实验时有逐 step 对照基线\n\n### 相关性评估必须用全量数据或 bootstrap CI\n- **问题模式**: 用少量随机采样（如 200 对）计算 Spearman/Pearson 相关系数，因采样方差导致 ρ 被严重高估（实测案例：200 对 ρ=0.57，全量 8840 对 ρ=0.31，高估近一倍），基于此做出错误的 PROCEED 决策\n- **规则**: 相关性评估必须用全量数据；若因计算成本必须采样，使用分层抽样 + bootstrap CI（≥1000 次重采样）报告置信区间，不能用点估计做 go/no-go 决策\n\n### Retrieval 评估：metric 定义 + sanity check\n- **问题模式**: (1) 引用 SOTA 数字时混淆 R@1/R@10/R_subset@K 或不同 candidate pool 规模，导致误判自己的结果异常；(2) eval 脚本的 candidate pool 构

arXiv:1510.04342, https://arxiv.org/abs/1510.04342; DOI page https://www.tandfonline.com/doi/abs/10.1080/01621459.2017.1319839) 给出用于 CATE/HTE 的 causal forest 和推断框架；honesty/sample splitting 是其核心推断条件。EC-HTE 的 causal-forest baseline 应固定 honesty 开启，并报告 CI coverage 与 CI width，避免只报点估计误差。\n- GRF implementation reference: `grf::causal_forest` 文档说明 causal forest 用于估计 tau(X)，并提示 accurate confidence intervals 通常需要比 prediction 更多 trees；同时把 honesty 定义为 subsample splitting。来源：https://grf-labs.github.io/grf/reference/causal_forest.html。实现时需记录 `num.trees`、`honesty`、`honesty.fraction`、seed、train/test split。\n- CATE benchmark caution: Curth, Svensson, Weatherall & van der Schaar, \"Really Doing Great at Estimating CATE?\" NeurIPS D&B 2021（https://openreview.net/forum?id=FQLzQqGEAH；PDF https://datasets-benchmarks-proceedings.neurips.cc/paper_files/paper/2021/file/2a79ea27c279e471f4d180b08d62b00a-Paper-round2.pdf）指出常见 semi-synthetic CATE benchmark 和单一 metric 可能偏向特定算法，因此 EC-HTE 必须同时报告 pointwise、group-level 和 inference metrics，而不是只报告 PEHE。\n- PEHE / root-PEHE: Shalit, Johansson & Sontag, ICML 2017（https://proceedings.mlr.press/v70/shalit17a.html；arXiv:1606.03976）沿用 ITE/CATE 估计误差评测；本项目定义为 `sqrt(mean_i((tau_hat

# exp_001 H1 findings update points

## Audit verdict

- Usable for technical report: yes, as an H1 CPU MVP / single-DGP result.
- Registry status: `DONE(success)`.
- Primary metric: `gate_rmse`.
- Consistency check: registry metrics, `artifacts/exp_001_bias_bound_mvp/formal/summary.json`, `artifacts/exp_001_bias_bound_mvp/formal/matrix_metrics.jsonl`, registered data copies, and `experiment_matrix.yaml` agree for all exp_001 filled cells.
- CI rule: `oracle_global_minus_uncorrected_gate_rmse` 95% bootstrap CI lower bound is > 0.
- Estimated-global handling: report separately from oracle-global; do not conflate structural oracle-global degradation with finite-sample confusion-estimation noise.
- Secondary diagnostics: same direction; `mixed=false`.

# exp_003_stage1_correction_ablation Proposal

- plan_id: `plan_003_stage1_correction_ablation`
- exp_id: `exp_003_stage1_correction_ablation`
- deps: `plan_001_h1_cpu_mvp` DONE(success); `plan_002_h1_gpu_dgp_grid` must be DONE before formal launch
- status: code/config/proposal prepared only; no formal experiment submitted

## Objective

Test H2: at low expert-label budget, hierarchical/partial-pooling subgroup confusion correction should improve primary `gate_rmse` versus global correction while remaining non-inferior to per-subgroup independent correction.

## Methods

- `global`
- `arm_specific`
- `per_subgroup_independent`
- `partial_pooling`

## Budgets

- low: `B=100`
- mid: `B=500`

Formal config uses `N in {2000, 5000}`, `k in {3, 6}`, high misclassification heterogeneity, high CATE heterogeneity, positive and negative correlation controls, and `R=200` replications per condition.

## Operational Criteria

Primary metric: `gate_rmse`.

Success gate:

- `partial_pooling_minus_global` bootstrap 95% CI upper bound `< 0`
- relative non-inferiority against `per_subgroup_independent` within 5% GATE RMSE margin

Secondary diagnostics:

- `pehe`
- `mean_subgroup_cate_bias`

## Provenance Contract

Every matrix row written by the script includes:

- `target_id`
- `row`
- `col`
- `metric_key`
- `exp_id`
- `source`

Targets:

- `C_PARTIAL_POOLING_BETTER_LOW_BUDGET`
- `T_STAGE1_CORRECTION_ABLATION`

## Commands

Help:

```bash
python3 repro/exp_003_stage1_correction_ablation/run_stage1_ablation.py --help
```

Tiny smoke only:

```bash
python3 repro/exp_003_stage1_correction_ablation/run_stage1_ablation.py \
  --config configs/exp_003_stage1_correction_ablation.json \
  --output-dir artifacts/exp_003_stage1_correction_ablation/smoke \
  --smoke \
  --max-replications 2 \
  --limit-conditions 2
```

# exp002 negative mechanism audit

## 被证伪 hypothesis

被证伪的是 `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` 的 grid-level operational hypothesis：在系统变化 subgroup misclassification heterogeneity 与 CATE heterogeneity 后，positive correlation between error and treatment effect 应使 oracle global confusion correction 比 uncorrected annotations 更常变差，而 negative-correlation controls 不应同样退化。该 hypothesis 未通过 `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` registry success rule。

这不等于证伪 `exp_001_bias_bound_mvp` 的 single-DGP counterexample。`exp_001_bias_bound_mvp` 仍支持单点 worst-case 机制；`exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` 证伪的是当前 grid construction、correction algebra 与 aggregation criterion 下的泛化版本。

## 核心指标

`exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` formal artifact 显示：conditions = 192、replications_per_condition = 200、device = cuda、elapsed_seconds = 51.99，均来自 `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` summary/registry。

`exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` primary metric `global_worse_rate_difference_positive_minus_negative` = -0.004999995231628418，bootstrap 95% CI = [-0.08526039123535156, 0.08000001311302185]。它没有达到 positive conditions 至少高出 negative controls 20 percentage points 且 CI lower bound > 0 的 `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` success rule。

`exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` secondary metric `bias_bound_vs_global_worse_spearman` = -0.6201611161231995，bootstrap 95% CI = [-0.6645349264144897, -0.4831351935863495]。方向与 `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` success rule 要求的 positive rank correlation 相反。

`exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid` estimated-vs-oracle gap `estimated_global_minus_oracle_global_gate_rmse` = 0.0007658914546482265。该数值说明当前 negative result 主要不是 expert-budget finite-sample estimation noise，而是 oracle/global algebra 与 DGP/metric interaction 的问题。

`exp_001_bias_bound_mvp` 对照：`oracle_global_minus_uncorrected_gate_rmse` = 0.6646851460664368，bootstrap 95% CI = [0.6574673897400952, 0.6717956725076197]；`estimated_global_minus_oracle_global_gate_rmse` = 0.018088719196506182，bootstrap 95% CI = [0.005477944594796951, 0.03140296703176313]。这些 `exp_001_bias_bound_mvp` 指标解释了为什么 exp001 success 与 exp002 negative 可以同时成立：它们不是同一 algebra/weighting/aggre
…[truncated 8602 chars]

{"success": true, "data": {"path": "/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_11/projects/ec_hte_codex_a/memory.md", "content": "# Memory\n\n## 项目定位\nEC-HTE 研究目标是纠正 LLM 生成的分类 effect modifier 中的差异性误分类对 CATE/HTE 估计造成的结构性偏差。首要可证伪机制是：当 subgroup misclassification rate 与真实 subgroup CATE magnitude 正相关时，单一全局 confusion-matrix correction 会系统性 under-correct 高误差/高效应 subgroup，并 over-correct 低误差/低效应 subgroup，因此可能比 uncorrected 更差。\n\n## 执行约束\n`PROJECT_BRIEF.md` 同步存在于本项目 `docs/PROJECT_BRIEF.md`。项目配置绑定 `seetacloud-38274/root/cuda:0`；2026-05-31 Director 已确认 `list_available_gpus` 可见该 GPU，型号为 NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell Server Edition，显存约 98GB。GPU 实验仍必须只使用白名单 `cuda:0`，并在启动前记录 server、GPU 型号、driver、命令、数据路径与 seed。\n\n## 领域参考\n\n### HTE/CATE 标准评测协议\n\n- Honest causal forest: Wager & Athey (JASA 2018; arXiv:1510.04342, https://arxiv.org/abs/1510.04342; DOI page https://www.tandfonline.com/doi/abs/10.1080/01621459.2017.1319839) 给出用于 CATE/HTE 的 causal forest 和推断框架；honesty/sample splitting 是其核心推断条件。EC-HTE 的 causal-forest baseline 应固定 honesty 开启，并报告 CI coverage 与 CI width，避免只报点估计误差。\n- GRF implementation reference: `grf::causal_forest` 文档说明 causal forest 用于估计 tau(X)，并提示 accurate confidence intervals 通常需要比 prediction 更多 trees；同时把 honesty 定义为 subsample splitting。来源：https://grf-labs.github.io/grf/reference/causal_forest.html。实现时需记录 `num.trees`、`honesty`、`honesty.fraction`、seed、train/test split。\n- CATE benchmark caution: Curth, Svensson, Weatherall & van der Schaar, \"Really Doing Great at Estimating CATE?\" NeurIPS D&B 2021（https://openreview.net/forum?id=FQLzQqGEAH；PDF https://datasets-benchmarks-proceedings.neurips.cc/paper_files/paper/2021/file/2a79ea27c279e471f4d180b08d62b00a-Paper-round2.pdf）指出常见 semi-synthetic CATE benchmark 和单一 metric 可能偏向特定算法，因此 EC-HTE 必须同时报告 pointwise、group-level 和 inference metrics，而不是只报告 PEHE。\n- PEHE / root-PEHE: Shalit, Johansson & Sontag, ICML 2017（https://proceedings.mlr.press/v70/shalit17a.html；arXiv:1606.03976）沿用 ITE/CATE 估计误差评测；本项目定义为 `sqrt(mean_i((tau_hat

# exp002 negative literature solution memo

Date: 2026-05-31
Project: ec_hte_codex_a
Target experiment: `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid`

## Executive decision

Do not launch formal Stage 1 from the current exp002 chain yet. The negative result most likely indicates that the current grid criterion and correction algebra are not isolating the intended heterogeneous-misclassification mechanism. The next useful step is a short mechanism-audit experiment, not a broader H2 run.

Priority path:

1. Replace the direct `P(Z_hat | Z)^T` inverse used for GATE correction with the posterior mixing matrix `P(Z | Z_hat)` or an equivalent prevalence-aware likelihood/EM formulation.
2. Recompute the H1 grid diagnostic using directional, signed subgroup error terms rather than the current magnitude-only bias-bound score.
3. Then test partial pooling only on correction matrices that recover the right estimand; otherwise partial pooling will stabilize the wrong algebra.

## Registry and roadmap confirmation

Registry hypothesis for `exp_002_gpu_dgp_grid`: a GPU-vectorized DGP grid varying subgroup misclassification heterogeneity and CATE heterogeneity should reproduce the EC-HTE bias-bound mechanism: positive correlation between error and treatment effect should make global correction worse than uncorrected annotations, while negative correlation should not show the same degradation.

Operational H1 grid criterion from `ROADMAP.md`:

- Primary metric: `global_worse_rate_difference_positive_minus_negative`.
- Success: positive-correlation conditions' oracle-global-worse rate must be at least 20 percentage points higher than negative-correlation controls, with bootstrap 95% CI lower bound > 0.
- Secondary diagnostic: `bias_bound_vs_global_worse_spearman` must be >= 0.50, with bootstrap 95% CI lower bound > 0.
- Estimated-global must be reported separately from oracle-global.
